Imiqondo
 
 Agasti 2026 
 
 
 
Amanothi angezansi ayirekhodi eliqhubekayo lemibono nokuqonda okwenzeke kimi ngokuhamba kwesikhathi. Ayivame ukucatshangwa ngokuphelele noma ukufezekiswa, nakuba esikhathini esizayo angase abe njalo. Noma mhlawumbe cha, kweminye imicabango ifomu lenothi lanele. Eminye imigwaqo efile ngakho-ke akufanele ukuqhubeka nokuyithuthukisa. Kodwa noma kunjalo, kungase kufanele ukurekhodwa.
 
 2023-02-24:  IZIGLITCH ZEZIHLUNGI ZEZINZWA
 
Ngiqaphele ngephutha ngesikhathi ngisebenza ukuthi uma ngizama ukukhulisa isithombe ku-Photoshop kusetshenziswa i-Neural Filters futhi isithombe sise-CMYK color profile futhi siqukethe izingxenye ezinkulu ezisobala, kwenzeka i-glitch eyinqaba, kodwa ngokuvamile ebukeka kahle.

 2023-10-04:  IZINDAWO ZEZAKHI ZOFUZO
 
Nguqule izilandelana zofuzo zaba imidwebo kusetshenziswa umthetho olula: U-A usho isinyathelo esiya enyakatho, T/U eningizimu, u-C empumalanga no-G entshonalanga. Ukulandelana kwezisekelo ngaleyo ndlela kunquma ukuma komugqa oqhubekayo.

 2024-03-22:  ISIGQI ESIBONAKALAYO SOMSEBENZI WEKHOMPYUTHA
 
Ngenze uhlelo olurekhoda konke ukushaya kwekhibhodi nokuchofoza kwegundane ngesikhathi ngisebenza kukhompyutha. Isikhathi siqhubeka ngendlela yokubhala evamile, ukusuka kwesobunxele kuye kwesokudla nokusuka phezulu kuye phansi. Iphuzu elimnyama lisho okufakiwe okurekhodiwe, indawo emhlophe isho ukungabikho kwayo.

 2024-08-19:  IZINKONDLO ZIKA-KUSHÍK
 
Bengisebenza kukhompyutha, kodwa bengiphazanyiswa njalo yigundane lami u-Kushík. Wayekhuphuka kimi, efuna ukunakwa, ekhuphuka etafuleni lami, ekhuphuka kukhompyutha. Waqala ukungididanisa kancane. Ngakho ngathukuthela, ngamvulela idokhumenti ye-MS Word engenalutho futhi ngamtshela okuthile mayelana nokuthi makadale uma efuna ukukwenza esikhundleni sami.
 Ngakho-ke u-Kushík waqala ukugijima ekhibhodi.
 
Kwadaleka le dokhumenti: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Ngenze uhlelo lwami lokubuka nokuhlela izithombe ze-thermographic zekhamera yami ye-Seek Thermal RevealPRO. Angizange ngithole isofthiwe efanelekile yale khamera ye-Windows, okuyihlazo, ngoba amafayela ayo e-thermographic aqukethe idatha yokushisa engasetshenziswa ngokuqhubekayo.
 
Uhlelo ngilubize ngokuthi i-Thermozedam, ngoba nganelisekile ngomklamo walo futhi ukulisebenzisa kuyalutha kimi. Uhlelo lulayisha amafayela e-TIFF e-thermographic, luvumela ukushintsha imephu yombala yesithombe kusetshenziswa i-colormap evela kulabhulali ye-matplotlib, ukukala izinga lokushisa elimaphakathi lesithombe, ukunquma imodi yokushisa, ukubonisa izinga lokushisa elincane nelikhulu, i-histogram, nangemva kokuchofoza, izinga lokushisa le-pixel ethile. Lapho ulondoloza, ingakwazi ukuthumela ifayela le-PNG elishintshiwe umbala futhi ngokuzithandela ilikhulise kusetshenziswa i-interpolation.
 
Isithombe-skrini sohlelo: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Ikhodi yomthombo: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  UKUKHANYA EBUNYIMENI
 
Bengicabanga ukwenza iphrojekthi ye-Arduino lapho ngingabeka khona i-laser ebhokisini elingenalutho (elibulawa amagciwane kusengaphambili kusetshenziswa isibani se-UV-C). Leyo laser yayizodubula i-pulse yesekhondi ngesikhathi esingahleliwe (ukuze ngingazi ukuthi kwenzeke nini ngempela, kodwa ngazi ukuthi kuzokwenzeka mhlawumbe phakathi nemizuzu engu-10 ku-100%). Uma ngingakwazi ukukwenza kahle, lowo mfutho wokukhanya wawungeke ubonwe muntu.
 
 2026-01-31:  UKULINGISWA KWEKHAMERA YE-PINHOLE
 
Bengizama ukudala ikhamera ye-pinhole elula esebenzayo ku-Python. Esikoleni esiphakeme, ngake ngadala eyodwa yangempela, kodwa kwabonakala kimi ukuthi uma ngiyilingisa, ngizoqonda kangcono ukusebenza kwayo, ngoba akwanele nje ukwenza imbobo ebhokisini, faka iphepha elizwela ukukhanya, bese ulithuthukisa ngemva kokuvezwa futhi uvumele i-physics yenze umsebenzi wayo (noma ngabe isebenza kanjani). Lapha, ngokuphambene, i-physics nayo kufanele ilingiswe.
 Bengizama ukwenza ukulingisa kube ngokoqobo ngangokunokwenzeka ngokwe-algorithm, nakuba umkhawulo, kunjalo, uhlala ulwazi lwami namandla okubala atholakalayo. Uhlelo, isibonelo, lusebenzisa i-forward ray tracing, okungukuthi ukulandelela ukukhanya kusuka emthonjeni kuye esigcawini nakukhamera, esikhundleni se-backward ray tracing evamile, elandelela imisebe ukusuka kukhamera ibuyele esigcawini. Indlela ye-forward iseduze nesiqondiso somzimba sokusabalala kokukhanya, kodwa ivame ukungasebenzi kahle ngokwezibalo.
 
Izibonelo zezilungiselelo zohlelo nencazelo yamapharamitha: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Ikhodi yomthombo: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  UKUHLELWA KWEZITHOMBE ZAMA-PIXEL
 
Ngizame ukuhlela ama-pixel ezithombe kusetshenziswa i-algorithm. Umbono wawuwukuhlela ama-pixel ngokwe-HUE nokukhanya: ku-axis eyodwa (mhlawumbe x) hlela ama-pixel ngokwe-HUE nakwelinye i-axis (mhlawumbe y) ngokukhanya. Lokhu kuhlela kabusha ama-pixel esithombe, kodwa awekho anyamalalayo noma adaliwe amasha. Noma kunjalo, izithombe eziphumayo zilahlekelwa incazelo yazo yasekuqaleni, kodwa zigcina umoya wemibala.
 
Ikhodi yomthombo: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  UKUHLOLA OKUNGALINDELEKILE KWE-INTERNET
 
Cishe amasonto amabili bengizinikele ekuhloleni i-inthanethi ngokungahleliwe, ngoba bengifuna ukwazi ukuthi ibukeka kanjani uma inganconywa ama-algorithm. Bengizama izindlela ezahlukene.
 
Eyokuqala (eyisiphukuphuku) kwakuwukudala uhlelo lokufaka nokuvula ama-URL akhiqizwa ngokungahleliwe. Lokho kwakungasebenzi kahle kakhulu, isikhala sama-URL angenzeka sikhulu kakhulu futhi akhona ambalwa kakhulu.
 
Indlela yesibili kwakuwukuzakhela owami i-crawler futhi nginikeze i-seed URL eyinqaba (mhlawumbe iwebhusayithi kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Ngabe sengizama ukuhlunga imiphumela kusetshenziswa i-LLM bese ngibuka kuphela lezo ezithakazelisayo (ngokwezindlela ezithile ezichaziwe). Ngithole izinto eziningi eziyinqaba zokuzijabulisa, kodwa akukho okuguquguqukayo.
 
Indlela yesithathu yayithela kakhulu futhi ijabulisa kakhulu. Ngithole ukuthi kukhona i-Certstream. Kuyisevisi ewusizo esakaza bukhoma izitifiketi zokuphepha ezikhishwe njengamanje emhlabeni jikelele. Ngakho-ke ibonisa ama-URL amawebhusayithi asanda kudalwa noma avuselela isitifiketi sawo.
 
Ikhodi yomthombo: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Ngichithe izinsuku ezimbalwa ngichofoza lawa ma-URL ngokungahleliwe. Ngivame ukuhlangabezana nokukhwabanisa (i-cryptocurrency, njll.), i-porn, i-AI, i-AI porn, iziqondiso zokuthi ungayenza kanjani i-AI porn, ukugembula, izindawo zokungena zezinhlelo, amawebhusayithi azelwe (anokuqukethwe okuyindawo noma imiyalezo ethi "sisebenza kukho").
 
Ngenxa yale nqubo, ngahlangabezana newebhusayithi treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], eyahlala enkumbulweni yami ngenxa yokubukeka kwayo okuncane nokungabikho kokuthuthukiswa kokunakwa kombukeli. Futhi ngithole ukuthi leyo webhusayithi iyinxenye encane kakhulu ye-inthanethi, ngoba ayikafakwa kuhlu ngisho yi-Google. I-Internet Archive yayitholile, kodwa kusuka kumalogi e-CT, njengami. Ukusesha ekugcineni kwathela izithelo.
 
 2026-05-06:  UMBUKISO E-PACIFIC
 
Bengicabanga ukudala izithombe ze-panoramic ezi-360° ze-Pacific ezikhiqizwe kusetshenziswa i-AI. Bese ufaka ikhodi ye-QR kuzo kusetshenziswa i-AI (okuthile ngesitayela se-Illusion Diffusion), ekhomba kubuciko obuthile. Ngemuva kwalokho, bekungaba nokwenzeka ukufaka imethadatha yendawo yamanga (mhlawumbe izixhumanisi ze-GPS phakathi ne-Pacific) bese uyilayisha ku-Google Maps.
 
Ngenye indlela, bekungaba nokwenzeka ukuthatha izindawo zangempela ezithathwe ngekhamera engu-360°, mhlawumbe kusuka edolobheni elilodwa, bese ushintsha izikhundla zazo ze-GPS ziye e-Pacific. Isizathu sokuthi ngikhuluma kuphela ngezithombe ezingu-360° ukuthi i-Google isavumela ukulayishwa ezindaweni eziqondile ze-GPS kusetshenziswa imethadatha. Ngenkathi izithombe ezijwayelekile kufanele zabelwe indawo ekhona.
 
 2026-06-04:  IZILONGO ZOMBALA
 
Bengizama ukufeza le phrojekthi, kodwa ngenxa yokudla isikhathi, angizange ngiyiqede. Kuze kube manje, ngibhale kuphela izandla, nginqume izinduna ezithathiwe (isizinda esisobala), ngagcina isikali sazo futhi ngenza uhlelo lokuzihlela kugridi. Nokho, lo ngumbhalo obufanele uchaze umqondo:
 
"Ngo-2026 ngibhale yonke imitholampilo yesikhumba ebonakalayo noma ukungajwayelekile kwemithambo ebusweni bomzimba wami. Isikali sagcinwa entweni ngayinye. Ngemuva kwalokho, izilonda ngazinye zanqunywa ngedijithali futhi zahlelwa kugridi evamile."
 
 2026-06-21:  UHLELO OLUNOKUHLAKANIPHA OLUZISHINTSHAYO
 
Bengizama ukudala uhlelo olusebenzisa i-OpenAI API nama-LLM abo. Uhlelo lunomsebenzi wokuzishintsha uqobo bese luziqala kabusha. Futhi bengifuna ukwazi ukuthi lokho kuzoholela kuphi.
 Ngeshwa, imodeli yayivame ukwenqaba umsebenzi ngoba yayiwubheka njengoyingozi. Noma yawufeza, kodwa olandelayo akazange. I-attractor yayihlale iwukwenqaba ngenxa yezizathu zokuphepha. Akazange afike ngisho nasesigabeni sesine.
 
Ikhodi yomthombo: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  UHLELO OLUNEZINHLELO EZINGAFANI
 
Uhlelo lwethu lwe-physics luyisicefe ngomqondo wokuthi kubonakala sengathi izinhlayiya eziyisisekelo ziyafana. I-electron njenge-electron, i-photon njenge-photon.
 
Kodwa ngingathanda ukudala ukulingisa kohlelo olunenqwaba yezinhlayiya eziyisisekelo, kodwa azikho ezimbili zazo ezingashintshwa. Ekugcineni, ngifikile ekuhlukeni lapho izinhlayiya ngokwazo zingenazo izakhiwo noma ubuwena. Zichazwa kuphela ngokusebenzisana kwazo, okuyinto, ngokombono wombukeli ngaphakathi kohlelo, cishe okulingana.
 
Imiphumela yalo mbono ngiyakujabulela ukuyicabanga. Uma ngikuthatha ngokweqile, ukuhlala ohlelweni olunjalo bekuyoba nzima kakhulu ukwenza izibikezelo. Futhi izinhlangano ezihlala ohlelweni olunjalo mhlawumbe bekuyodingeka ziqaphele ukulahlekelwa noma ukwebiwa kwezinhlayiya zazo.
 
Bengizama ukuyilingisa futhi ngokobuchwepheshe ngiwufezile umsebenzi. Nokho, bekungewona umphumela othakazelisa kangako. Ngokwami, lokhu kungenxa yokuthi ukuze ubuke ezinye izakhiwo ezivelayo ezithakazelisayo, kudingeka amandla amaningi okubala (izinhlayiya eziningi, isikhathi esiningi ekulingiseni, mhlawumbe izilinganiso eziningi zokulingisa) nokuhlaziya okujulile kokukhiphayo.
 
Isibuyekezo kusuka ku-2026-08-22: Ngesikhathi ngibhekabheka imihlaba ekhiqiziwe, ekugcineni ngahlangabezana nezakhiwo ezizihlelayo ezimangalisayo. Ziyindilinga kuya ku-oval futhi zibukeka kancane njengama-lipid capsules. Zinomngcele ocacile wangaphandle nangaphakathi, kuyilapho umgoqo noma "ulwelwesi" olwenza ingaphandle lazo luzinzile kakhulu, kuyilapho ingaphakathi lazo lihamba kakhulu. Lo mbewu ngiyibeke njengenani elizenzakalelayo kudemo engezansi.
 
Qaphela: ukuthuthukiswa kohlelo, ngaleyo ndlela usayizi walezi zakhiwo, kuncike ekulungisweni kwesibonisi. Isivinini se-animation sithinta ukuzinza kwazo, mhlawumbe ngenxa yokungacacile kwezibalo ngezinyathelo zesikhathi ezinkulu. Ngisho noma izakhiwo zibhidlika, isibonelo ngemva kokwandisa isivinini se-animation, uhlelo ekugcineni luzozihlela kabusha futhi lwakhe ezintsha.
 
Lokhu akuyona into ebengihlose ukuyidala. Ngidale kuphela uhlelo olunezinhlayiya ezihlukile částicemi/interakcemi futhi lokhu kwavela njengento evelayo. Kuhle, ngokuzumayo ngine-simulator ye-"lipids".
 
Idemo esebenzayo: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  UDLALA
 
Kuthiwani uma ngingathatha isithombe sayo yonke imfucuza engiyiphonsa emgqonyeni? Kungathathwa isithombe sayo nesikali, bese imfucuza isikwa isuswe ngemuva, bese ihlelwa ngobukhulu obufanele kugridi evamile.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Ngesikhathi ngithintana ne-ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) mayelana nendlela elula yokuchaza ucwaningo lwe-OPTICAL RHYTHM OF COMPUTER WORK (bheka ngenhla), yathi ngokuzenzakalelayo leyo ngxoxo ithi "Incazelo yohlelo  天天中彩票买 ". Nokho, leyo ngxoxo yayisanda kudalwa ngenxa nje yokuchaza lolu cwaningo. Okuqukethwe kohlelo engikuthumele kuyo kwakujwayelekile ngokuphelele.
 
Ngakho-ke, nganquma ukuyibhekana nalokho futhi ngabhala lokhu:
 
"Kungani uyiqambe le ngxoxo ngokuthi Incazelo yohlelo  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Impendulo yayo yaba:
 
"Angazi. 😭 Lowo mlingiswa othi „զ“ uyinhlamvu yesi-Armenia ethi „za“, ngakho-ke „Incazelo yohlelo զ“ ayisho lutho nhlobo ngesiCzech. Izihloko zezingxoxo zikhiqizwa ngokuzenzakalelayo kusukela ekuqaleni kwengxoxo, futhi lapha umkhiqizi wesihloko usenqume ukufaka uhlamvu olungahleliwe oluvela kulwimi lwesi-Armenia. Angiboni sizathu salokho kokuqukethwe kwengxoxo. Ngakho cha, akusona uphawu oluyimfihlo lohlelo. Kumane kuwukuthi „Incazelo yohlelo“ + umhlaseli wezinhlamvu."
 
Ngemva kokunye ukusebenzisana okumbalwa, yaqaphela ukuthi cha, akusilo isi-Armenia, kodwa isiShayina. Noma kunjalo, kuyabonakala ukuthi ekuqaleni yayididekile kakhulu. Ngicabanga ukuthi umucu othi „ 天天中彩票买 “ usebenza kancane njengophawu oludumile oluthi „ SolidGoldMagikarp “. Angisho ukuthi usebenza ngendlela efanayo, futhi angazi noma i-tokenizer imele umucu othi „ 天天中彩票买 “ njengophawu olulodwa. Noma kunjalo, imodeli iziphatha kancane njenge-ChatGPT 3 ngophawu oluthi „ SolidGoldMagikarp “ lapho isebenza ngalo. Futhi akukhohlwa ukuthi kungenzeka kuhlobene nenkumbulo yayo, ngoba ifinyelela ngaphandle kwale ngxoxo.
 
Yonke le ngxoxo ithunyelwe njenge-PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  INKINOBHO YAMAPHUPHO AMI
 
Ngicasulwa njalo ngamawindi ezingxoxo ze-Windows anikeza izinto ezifana nokwenza ikhophi yasenqolobaneni nge-OneDrive, izibuyekezo, nokunye okunjalo. Ahlale enikeza ukukhetha phakathi kokuthi "Qala" kanye "Khumbuza kamuva". Lokho kuyadumaza kakhulu uma ngingenawo umdlandla kunoma yikuphi kwalokhu kukhetha.
 
Ngakho-ke ngacabanga ukudala isithombe (i-photomontage) lapho ngizokwengeza khona inketho yesithathu - inkinobho yamaphupho ami, okudabukisayo ukuthi ayikho ohlelweni okwamanje. Kungaba inkinobho ethi "Hamba uye esihogweni". Ngibheja ukuthi angiyena yedwa umuntu lo mfanekiso ongathinta kakhulu kuye.
 
 2026-09-18:  ISIBALI-MANANI ESINOKUHLAKANIPHA OKUPHEZULU
 
Ngicabange okunye ukungasebenzi okuyisiwula enginomuzwa wokuthi kufanele ngikuhlolisise ngokwethiyori.
 
Kuyinhlelo ezosebenza njengesibali-manani esiyisisekelo kakhulu. Kungaba ukwazi ukuhlanganisa izinombolo eziphelele ezinhle, akukho okunye. Nokho, umehluko ukuthi umphumela ngeke ubalwe ngokujwayelekile endaweni, kodwa esikhundleni salokho uzothunyelwa kumodeli yolimi ethuthuke kakhulu (isb. ChatGPT-6 Astra). Futhi umsebenzi wayo kungaba ukubala leso sibonelo esilula futhi ibuyisele umphumela kuphela.
 
Kuyangihlekisa kakhulu ukusebenzisa imodeli yolimi ethuthuke kakhulu entweni eyisisekelo kakhulu. Empeleni, mhlawumbe kuwukuhlekisa ngokuthi izinto eziyisisekelo ziphathiswa kanjani i-AI namuhla nokuthi ukumaketha kubeka kanjani ilebula ethi "AI" kukho konke njengohlobo oluthile lwemilingo yokusebenza.
 
Ngokusebenza, kuzoba kubi kakhulu kunesibali-manani esijwayelekile, singathembeki, sibe nokubambezeleka okukhulu, sibize kakhulu, singabi nemvelo, sidinge uxhumano lwe-inthanethi, ...

============================================================
Izifihla-buso
 
 u-Agasti 2026 
 
 
 
Izithombe ezingezansi yilezo masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] ezidalwe ku-Adobe Photoshop phakathi nomsebenzi wami kumaphrojekthi ahlukahlukene. Isifihla-buso ngasinye sasigcinwa njengesithombe esihlukile ngesikhathi sidaliwe. Igalari yalezi zithombe ayiwona umphumela wokukhetha. Uma isifihla-buso sidalwe phakathi nomsebenzi, siyingxenye yaso. Izithombe zihlelwe ngokulandelana kwesikhathi kusukela kwesidala kuye kwesisha.

============================================================
Izintwala
 
 uMeyi 2026 
 
 
 
Unyaka wonke ngithela izinsalela zetiye elivuthululiwe kanye namanzi asasele endaweni eyodwa otshanini e-Plzeň-Božkov izikhathi eziningi ngosuku, ngoba angifuni ukuvala amapayipi.
 
Kule ndawo izinkumba eziyisikhombisa zaqala ukubuthana, zona ezidla amaqabunga etiye.
 
Kwesinye isikhathi uthola ishumi nambili kuzo lapho, kwesinye isikhathi akukho neyodwa.
 
Ngabelana nabo ngokuthandayo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg | izinkumba netiye]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Isihloko se-Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Isihloko se-Metaweb
 
 u-Ephreli 2026 
 
 
 
IMANIFESTO
 
Le webhusayithi iyiphrojekthi emile egxile ekulondolozeni okuqukethwe isikhathi eside.
 
Konke okuqukethwe kugcinwe kumafayela, ngaphandle kokuncika kumininingo-gcinwa (databases) noma izinhlelo zangaphandle. Iwebhusayithi evelayo ikhiqizwa njengomphumela omile, ongakwazi ukukopishwa kalula, ukugcinwa kungobo yomlando, nokwakhiwa kabusha.
 
Le phrojekthi isatshalaliswe ezinhlelweni eziningi ezizimele: izindawo zokugcina (repositories), izinsiza zokugcina kungobo yomlando, nezithombe ezimile (static snapshots). Lokhu kunciphisa ubungozi bokulahleka kwedatha.
 
Inhloso wukuqinisekisa ukuthi kungenzeka ukwakha kabusha okuqukethwe ngisho noma ezinye izingxenye zengqalasizinda yasekuqaleni ziyeka ukuba khona.
 
UNGQIMBA LOKUGCINA KUNGOBO YOMHLANDO KU-INTHANETHI
 
Lezi zixhumanisi zimelela iphrojekthi efanayo egcinwe ezinhlelweni ezahlukene nangezindlela ezahlukene.
 
Zihlanganisa indawo yokugcina eyinhloko (canonical source repository), amakhophi ayo, nezithombe ezimile eziphelele. Ngokuhlangene, zakha isakhiwo esiphindaphindwayo esiklanyelwe ukulondoloza iphrojekthi nokuvumela ukwakhiwa kwayo kabusha ngisho noma ezinye izingxenye zingasatholakali.
 
Le phrojekthi ikhona futhi njengephakethe le-Python CLI elisatshalaliswa nge-GitHub ne-PyPI, kuhlanganise nezithombe zokusekelayo (fallback snapshots) ezifakiwe zemibhalo ekhethiwe.
 
Ngakho-ke, ukugcinwa kwedijithali kwale webhusayithi kusebenzisa, ngaphezu kwesizinda sayo, izinsiza ze-GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku kanye ne-paste.rs.
 
[CODE BLOCK]

IMIBHALO YEZOBUFACHEDI
 
Imibhalo yezobuchwepheshe edingekayo ukuze kwakhiwe kabusha le webhusayithi.
 
Buka imibhalo ephelele yezobuchwepheshe [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
IPHAKETHE LOMTHOMBO LOKWAKHA KABUSHA
 
Iphakethe lomthombo lewebhusayithi likhiqizwa ngokuzenzakalelayo ngesikhathi sokwakha (build).
 
Ngaphakathi ephaketheni kukhona ikhodi yomthombo yale webhusayithi, imanifesto yezimpahla (assets), izibalo zokuhlola ze-SHA-256 kanye nombhalo we-Python ngaphandle kokuncika kwangaphandle. Ayiqukethe izimpahla ezinkulu ezivela ku-public/images/, public/files/ kanye ne-public/demos/, ukuze usayizi wewebhusayithi ebukhoma ungaphindaphindwa ngokungadingekile. Ngemva kokuvula, umbhalo uqala uzame ukukopisha izimpahla endaweni kusuka ekwakhiweni okuqediwe bese uzilanda ezindaweni ezibukwayo (mirrors) zomphakathi.
 
Ngakho-ke, ikhodi yomthombo ikhiqiza ukwakhiwa, futhi ukwakhiwa kukhiqiza ikhodi yomthombo.
 
Iphakethe lomthombo liyakwazi ukwakhiwa kabusha ngokuziphindaphinda: ngemva kokulanda izimpahla nokufaka okuncikile, iwebhusayithi entsha nephakethe lomthombo elisha lingakhiqizwa kabusha kulo.
 
Landa iphakethe lomthombo lewebhusayithi elikwazi ukwakhiwa kabusha (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
UKUTHEMBEKA NOKUHLONZEKA KOKWAKHIWA
 
I-SHA-256 iqinisekisa ukuthi amafayela okwakha awashintshanga. Ngaphezu kwalokho, isiginesha ye-OpenPGP ivumela ukuqinisekisa ukuthi imanifesto yezibalo zokuhlola isayinwe ngumbhali wale webhusayithi.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — uhlu lwezibalo zokuhlola ze-SHA-256 zamafayela okwakha
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — isiginesha ye-OpenPGP ehlukene yefayela SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — isibalo sokuhlola se-SHA-256 sefayela SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — incazelo efundeka ngomshini yokuthembeka kokwakhiwa
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — isimo esinolwazi sokusayina kokwakhiwa okuthile
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — ukhiye womphakathi we-OpenPGP wokuqinisekisa isiginesha
 
 I-Fingerprint yokhiye oyinhloko we-OpenPGP:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Akusiyo yonke imisebenzi (deployment) okufanele isayinwe. Ukwakhiwa okusayiniwe kufanele kuqukathe ifayela SHA256SUMS.txt.asc futhi isiginesha yalo kufanele iqinisekiswe ngempumelelo. Isitatimende esiku-integrity.json noma SIGNING_STATUS.txt asiwubufakazi besiginesha evumelekile; la mafayela anolwazi kuphela.
 
OKUTHUNYELWAYO (EXPORTS)
 
Iwebhusayithi ikhiqiza ukuthunyelwa kombhalo ocacile (plain-text export) wazo zonke izihloko ngaso sonke isikhathi sokwakha. Iqukethe okuqukethwe kombhalo okuthunyelwe ngendlela elandelanayo ngaphandle kokuncika ku-CSS, JavaScript, noma ubuchwepheshe obuthile bewebhu. Isebenza njengongqimba oluncane lokugcina kungobo yomlando lokukhomba, ukufunda ungaxhunyiwe ku-inthanethi, ukusesha umbhalo ogcwele, nokukwazi ukwakha kabusha okuqukethwe isikhathi eside ngisho noma kwenzeka ukuwohloka okuthile kwesakhiwo sasekuqaleni sewebhusayithi. Ukuthunyelwa kombhalo kufanelekile nakumamodeli olimi amakhulu.
 
Buka ukuthunyelwa kombhalo wazo zonke izihloko (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Ukuze kucutshungulwe ngomshini, kukhona ukuthunyelwa kwe-JSON-LD okwenziwa ngesandla, okuhlanganisa imibhalo yezihloko yesiCzech neyesiNgisi, imethadatha, nezixhumanisi zemidiya. Isebenzisa isichazamazwi esijwayelekile Schema.org esigcwaliswe ngemigomo yayo echazwe kusichazamazwi semethadatha sale webhusayithi [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Ngakho-ke igcina incazelo yezinkambu ngazinye kanye nobudlelwano phakathi kwezinguqulo zezilimi zezihloko.
 
Okuqukethwe kwewebhusayithi kungathunyelwa ngesandla nakufomethi ye-PDF, egcina okuqukethwe kwayo ngendlela efanelekile yokufunda, ukuphrinta, nokugcinwa kungobo yomlando isikhathi eside okuzimele kuwebhusayithi uqobo. Imininingwane mayelana nendlela yokukhiqiza kanye nesakhiwo sokuthunyelwa inikezwe emibhalweni yezobuchwepheshe ngenhla.
 
Ngokufanayo, ukuthunyelwa kwe-EPUB nakho kuyadalwa, okuhloselwe ikakhulukazi abafundi be-elekthronikhi (e-readers) nezinye izinhlelo zokusebenza ezisebenza ngezincwadi ze-elekthronikhi. Uma kuqhathaniswa ne-PDF, isebenzisa ukuhamba kombhalo okuguquguqukayo futhi imele okunye ukumelwa kokuqukethwe kwewebhusayithi.
 
Ukumelwa okuhlukile kokuqukethwe kwakhiwa futhi ukuthunyelwa kwe-SSTV kusukela ngomhla ka-10 Septhemba 2026, okuguqula ngokuzenzakalelayo okuqukethwe kombhalo nezithombe zewebhusayithi kube amakhasi athuthukiselwe ukudluliswa kwezithombe okujwayelekile kwe-Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) kumodi ye-PD120 (640 × 496 px). Amakhasi ngazinye afakwe njengokudluliswa komsindo ngefomethi ye-WAV futhi angabukwa emuva ngesamukeli se-SSTV esijwayelekile ngaphandle kwe-HTML yasekuqaleni noma isiphequluli sewebhu. Ukuthunyelwa okuphelele kunecala elingaba 21 GB namahora angu-61 omsindo.
 
Landa ukuthunyelwa kwe-SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Ngaphandle kokuthunyelwa okukhiqizwa ngomshini, kukhona nokuthunyelwa kwewebhusayithi ngomlomo okungamahora ayisithupha kusukela ngomhla ka-29 Agasti 2026, lapho ngihamba khona kancane kancane, ngifunda, futhi ngiphawula ngokuqukethwe kwayo ngamazwi ami. Ngakho-ke, ngaphandle kwemibhalo uqobo, ukuqoshwa kuthwebula umongo owengeziwe, izinkumbulo, izincazelo, nolwazi olungewona ngokuqondile ingxenye yewebhusayithi.
 
Dlala ukuthunyelwa kwewebhusayithi ngomlomo (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
UNGQIMBA LOKUGCINA KUNGOBO YOMHLANDO OKUNGAXHUNYIWE (OFFLINE)
 
Izingxenye ezikhethiwe zokuqukethwe kwewebhusayithi zikhona nakumathuluzi okugcina angobo yomlando ahlukene, ahlukaniswe ngokomzimba nengqalasizinda yenethiwekhi futhi ngaleyo ndlela akha ungqimba lokugcina kungobo yomlando olungaxhunyiwe (air-gapped). Lokhu kuhlanganisa amakhophi aphrintiwe e-analog kanye nemidiya yedatha ephathekayo. Amathuluzi ngazinye ahluka ngendlela yokufunda, ukuncika kwezobuchwepheshe, ukuphatha, ukutholakala, kanye nesikhathi sokuphila esilindelekile. Amanye afundeka ngokuqondile ngaphandle kwedivayisi ye-elekthronikhi, kuyilapho amanye edinga i-hardware ehambisanayo, isofthiwe, noma ukulamula kwesikhungo sokugcina kungobo yomlando.
 
Umugqa ngamunye werejista elandelayo ukhombisa ikhophi eyodwa ethile ebonakalayo.

Indawo yokulahla izinto ye-Clipboard
 
 u-Ephreli 2026 
 
 
 
Lo msebenzi usebenza nokuqukethwe kwesistimu ye-clipboard ye-Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Noma yimuphi umbhalo, isithombe, noma ifayela elikopishiwe, lifakwa ngokushesha endaweni eyodwa elinganiselwe.
 Okuqukethwe akuhlelwa noma kuhlungwe. Izinto ezithile zibekwa ngokungahleliwe kufomethi futhi ziyagqagqana.
 Indwangu igcwala kancane kancane ngaphandle kwethuba lokubuyela emuva noma ukuhlela.
 
Isithombe ngasinye esingezansi siwumphumela wosuku olulodwa futhi sithwebula ukuqhubeka komsebenzi wami kukhompyutha.

Ikhodi yomthombo
 Ikhodi yomthombo ye-Python yalo msebenzi itholakala lapha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Izinhlangano zesikhashana

LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

============================================================
Izinhlangano zesikhashana
 
 uJanuwari 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Lo msebenzi uwukumelela kobuciko bokuthi ubuchopho bukaBoltzmann bungavela kanjani [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] nezinye izinhlangano zesikhashana, ezivela okwesikhashana ku-entropy ngokulungiselelwa okungahleliwe kwezinhlayiya bese zinyamalala kuyo futhi.
 
Ezweni langempela lezomzimba, ukubuka lesi simo kudinga isikhathi eside ngokungenakucatshangelwa.
 
Ngakho-ke, ngishintshe ngamandla isivinini sezinhlayiya ngazinye ukuze kwenzeke ukukhombisa ukuvela nokunyamalala kwezinhlangano eziyikhulu ngesikhathi esifanele. Angizange ngiphazamise imizila yezinhlayiya nganoma iyiphi indlela.
 
Izinhlangano zakhiwe ngokukhetha amagama ayikhulu angahleliwe kusichazamazwi, ukunikeza isithombe esiwamelelayo kwelinye nelinye, bese ngikuvumela ukuba kuphinde kuvele emkhakheni we-entropic wezinhlayiya.
 
Kuwumzamo wokukhombisa ukuthi incazelo, isimo, noma umcabango ungavela kanjani okwesikhashana esiphithiphithini, ngaphandle kokuba nanoma yisiphi isicelo sokuba khona unomphela.

TITLE: I-genome ku-Theodore spiral

I-genome ku-Theodore spiral
 
 uJanuwari 2026 
 
 
 
Lokhu kubukwa kuguqula ulwazi lwezakhi zofuzo olulandelanayo (DNA/RNA) lube yisimo esiqhubekayo 2D se-Theodore spiral [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Iphuzu ngalinye ku-spiral lihambisana nesisekelo esisodwa ngokulandelana kwaso njengoba sikhona ngempela kulolu chungechunge. Ukuqala kochungechunge kusemaphakathi ne-spiral. Umbala wephuzu umele uhlobo lwesisekelo.
 
Le spiral ivumela ukuboniswa kwedatha ende 1D endaweni encane 2D, ngaphandle kokuphazamisa ukugeleza kolwazi. Azikho iziphazamiso ezenziweyo, njengokubuyela ekuqaleni komugqa, okuvame kakhulu ekumeleleni kombhalo.
 
Njengesibonelo, ngibone ngeso lengqondo ama-genome ezinto eziphilayo ezintathu ezinobude obuhlukahlukene. Zonke zine-genome elandelanayo, elungele kakhulu lolu hlobo lokubona.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Igciwane le-Ebola: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]

SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Ikhodi yomthombo
 Ikhodi yomthombo ye-Python yalo msebenzi itholakala lapha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ulimi-luhlobo
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Ulimi-luhlobo
 
 Disemba 2025 
 
 
 
Amamodeli olimi ajwayelekile akhetha ithokheni elilandelayo ngokukhetha okukodwa kokusikisela okungenzeka kakhulu, okuthi ngokwesilinganiso sawo sezibalo kulingane kahle kumongo. Lokhu sizokubiza ngokuthi ukulinganisa okujwayelekile.
 Le phrojekthi yenza okuphambene nalokho.
 Esikhundleni sokukhetha amathokheni angenzeka kakhulu, siphoqa imodeli ukuba ifike kwalawo angenzeka kancane, okungukuthi, lawo ebingeke neze iwasebenzise kumodi evamile.
 Umphumela uba yi-anti-jazyk – umbhalo owakhiwe ezingxenyeni ezingaphansi, ezijezisiwe, futhi ngokuvamile ezikude kakhulu zesilulumagama semodeli.
 Lokhu kudala izakhiwo ezingezona izimpawu ezingahleliwe nje, kodwa futhi ezingezona imisho enengqondo.
 
Kusebenza kanjani
 Imodeli ithola okokufaka okufushane (isb. "This is ").
 Ikhiqiza ukusatshalaliswa okungenzeka kwawo wonke amathokheni alandelayo angaba khona.
 Esikhundleni sokukhetha engxenyeni engenhla yokusatshalaliswa (amathokheni angenzeka kakhulu), wonke umshini uyaphendulwa futhi kukhethwa kusukela phansi (amathokheni angenzeka kancane).
 Lokhu kuveza "izingqimba ezingaphansi" zemodeli: imishwana engavamile, izingcezu zolimi, nezinye izinto imodeli evame ukuzicindezela.
 
Ikhodi
 Ikhodi ingaqhutshwa ku-Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
I-Colab lapha isebenza ikakhulukazi njengendawo yokusebenzela. Akuyona ikhophi eyinhloko yengobo yomlando yekhodi.
 Yingakho inqubo efanayo igcinwe nangezansi ngqo esihlokweni.
 
Ikhodi ihlukaniswe yaba amabhulokhi amathathu ahlukene njengaku-notebook. Owokuqala ulayisha imodeli, owesibili ukhiqiza umbhalo, kanti owesithathu ulondoloza okukhiphayo kokugcina.
 
1. Ukulayishwa kwemodeli ne-tokenizer
 Leli bhulokhi lidinga ukusebenza kanye kuphela. Lilayisha imodeli ye-GPT-2 ne-tokenizer.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Ukukhiqizwa kolimi-luhlobo
 Kuleli bhulokhi, umuntu angashintsha umbhalo wokufaka, inani lamathokheni amasha, nenani le-bottom_k.
 Uma i-bottom_k incane, imodeli iphoqwa kakhulu phansi kokusatshalaliswa okungenzeka.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Ukulondolozwa kokukhiphayo kokugcina
 Leli bhulokhi lilondoloza umbhalo wokugcina okhiqiziwe kufayela le-output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Izisindo zemodeli ye-GPT-2 aziyona ingxenye yewebhusayithi. Lapho iqalwa, ilandwa emthonjeni wangaphandle.
 Lapha kugcinwe inqubo ikakhulukazi: umhlahlandlela omncane wokuthi ungayiphoqa kanjani imodeli ukuthi ikhiphe imfucuza yezibalo esikhundleni solimi olujwayelekile.
 
Isibonelo sokukhiphayo
 Lapha ngisebenzise imodeli ye-GPT-2 futhi ngayivumela ukuba igcwalise umbhalo othi "This is ":

Ukuhlaziywa kwesibonelo sokukhiphayo
 Ulimi-luhlobo aluwona umsindo ongahleliwe. Uchungechunge lwezingcezu lukhumbuza imikhondo yezizinda zedatha ezithile imodeli okungenzeka yayibona, kodwa okuthi ukulinganisa okujwayelekile kungayibizi cishe neze, ngoba azinanzuzo ngokwezibalo. Ulimi-luhlobo, ngokuphambene, luyazikhetha.
 Ezinye izingxenye zombhalo ezikhiqizwa yimodeli zibukeka njengezinto ezivela kusethi yedatha yokuqeqesha. Kwesinye isikhathi umuntu angabona izinkomba zokuklwebha idatha yezitolo ezahlukahlukene ze-e-shop ("BuyableInstoreAndOnline", "SpecialOrderable", "ItemThumbnailImage"), izihloko ezingaba buzwela noma ezithinta umphakathi ("sexual", "Muslims", "Hispanic"), amagama esayensi noma izingxenye zawo ("isomer", "antidepress", "[m]icrobial", "[p]uncture", "intolerance"), izinkomba zamasiko ("Monroe", "Phelps", "Rockefeller", "Nitrome"), izinto ezingokwenkolo-zinganekwane noma eziyizinganekwane ("Archdemon", "devils"), izindawo ("Moroc", "Afric"), izingxenye zokusebenzisana komsebenzisi wewebhusayithi ("cloneembedreportprint", "IMAGES"), njll.
 Kuyadingeka ukwengeza imikhawulo yalokhu kuhlaziya: ubuchopho bunomkhuba wokukha amaphethini aziwayo (imikhiqizo, amagama, imiqondo) bese buwushaya indiva okunye. Ngaphezu kwalokho, amanye amagama anezincazelo ezimbili ("intolerance" ingaba yezokwelapha neyomphakathi), ngakho ukuhunyushwa kwawo akucaci.

Isibungu ε
 
 Septhemba 2025 
 
 
 
Incazelo:
 Isisekelo siwuhlelo lwezinzwa (neural network) oluvulekile lwesibungu i-C. elegans. Isisindo ngasinye wᵢ′ silungiswa kanje:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 lapho u-εᵢ eyiguquguquko engahleliwe ezimele enokusabalala okufanayo esikhathini esiphakathi kuka-[−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Isisusa:
 Ukungezwa kwenombolo encane engenakubalwa u-εᵢ akusebenzeli ukuthuthukisa imodeli. Kusebenzela kuphela ukwenza isibungu esichaziwe esingeke sikwazi ukubalwa ngokuphelele. Lowo ukuphela kwenjongo yaso.

============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Julayi 2024 
 
 
 
I-DullGPT iwucwaningo lwengqondo olukhombisa ukuthi kungenzeka ukudala uhlelo lobuhlakani bokwenziwa (AI) kusetshenziswa indlela elula kakhulu (kodwa engasebenzi kahle neze). Le phrojekthi iqukethe uhlelo olukhiqiza konke okungenayo okungenzeka, lubufake kumodeli ye-ChatGPT, bese lulondoloza izimpendulo ezitholiwe. Ngokuhamba kwesikhathi, lwakha isizindalwazi sokufakwayo nokukhiphayo, ngaleyo ndlela lwakhe inkohliso yohlelo oluhlakaniphile.
 
I-DullGPT inezindlela ezimbili: “ukufunda” kanye “nokusebenzisa”. Kumodi yokufunda, uhlelo lukhiqiza okufakwayo futhi luqoqe izimpendulo, kuyilapho kumodi yokusebenzisa, luvumela umsebenzisi ukuthi afake okufakwayo futhi athole izimpendulo kusizindalwazi.
 
Empeleni, lokhu kuyisibonelo esidlulele sendlela yokusebenzisa amandla amakhulu (brute-force) ukulingisa ubuhlakani. Esikhundleni sokuzama ukuqonda incazelo yokufakwayo nokukhiqiza izimpendulo ngokusekelwe kumodeli yokufunda, kumane kuqoqe futhi kulondoloze inani elikhulu kakhulu lezibhangqwana zokufakwayo-nokukhiphayo. Ngokombono wokuziphatha, kufanele kungahlukaniseki ne-ChatGPT uma inemininingwane eyanele. Lokhu kuphakamisa umbuzo wokuthi ingabe ukuqonda kwangempela nobuhlakani kuyadingeka yini ekudaleni ukuxhumana okuhlakaniphile. Ngamanye amazwi: I-DullGPT ikhiqiza izimpendulo ezingahlukaniseki nalezo ezikhiqizwa uhlelo lwangempela oluhlakaniphile, kodwa azinayo noma yiluphi uhlobo lokuhlangenwe nakho kwangempela noma ukuzihlola.
 
Yebo, i-DullGPT inemikhawulo eminingi uma iqhathaniswa ne-ChatGPT, ehlanganisa isivinini “sokuqeqeshwa” nokuncika kwayo kwamanye amamodeli amakhulu olimi, ukungacabangeli umlando wengxoxo, nokungacabangeli zonke izimpawu zokufakwayo (isib. izinhlamvu zesiShayina, njll.). Ngokungafani namamodeli amakhulu olimi, izohlala ikhipha okufanayo kokufakwayo okufanayo. Njengoba lokhu kuwucwaningo lwengqondo kuphela ngaphandle kokusetshenziswa okusebenzayo, akukho mqondo wokuzama ukulungisa lezi zinkinga.
 
Kodwa-ke, kufanele kuqashelwe ukuthi angizami ukuvikela impikiswano yegumbi laseShayina (Chinese room argument), ebuza amathuba okufinyelela ubuhlakani bangempela noma ukuqonda ngokusekelwe kuphela ekukhohliseni izimpawu ngaphandle kokuqonda kwangempela. Ngithi kuphela ukuthi okungenani uhlelo olulodwa olunjalo oluhlakaniphile lungadalwa, nakuba ngendlela engasebenzi kahle neyisiwula ngokuphelele. Kodwa angicabangi ukuthi wonke amasistimu kudingeka abe angenalutho ngaphakathi.
 
Nansi ikhodi yomthombo yalolu hlelo:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Imingcele yobumfihlo
 
 uFebruwari 2024 
 
 
 
Ngenze uhlelo olulula engingalunika umkhombandlela owodwa noma ngaphezulu. Ihlola konke okubandakanya amafolda angaphansi, futhi uma ithola isithombe (ngisho isithombe lapha ifayela eliphela nge-.jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), yengeza indlela yaso eqoqweni lazo zonke izithombe. Ngemva kokuqeda ukuhlola, ikhetha isithombe esisodwa kuphela kuzo bese isibonisa.
 
Ngemva kwalokho, ngixhume ku-computer yami wonke ama-hard drive anezipele zezithombe engiziqoqe phakathi nokuphila kwami (manje aqukethe ngaphezu kuka-91 000 wezithombe). Ngibe sengisetha wonke ama-hard drive njengamafolda okuzenzakalelayo lapho uhlelo lukhetha khona izithombe. Ngenxa yezizathu eziwusizo, kwakudingekile ukuvimbela ukukhethwa kwamanye amafolda, ngoba amanye ayequkethe idatha yezinhlelo kuphela, njengezithonjana zazo. Ngemva kokucindezela ukhiye u-ENTER, uhlelo lukhethe isithombe esingahleliwe empilweni yami. Ngemva kokucindezela u-ENTER futhi, uhlelo lukhethe esinye isithombe, njalonjalo...
 
Ezithombeni ezikhethwe ngokungahleliwe, ngenze uchungechunge olumbalwa olwalulandelana. Uchungechunge ngalunye luqala ngokukhethwa kwesithombe ngokungahleliwe futhi luqhubeke ngokukhetha ezinye izithombe ngokungahleliwe, kuze kube yilapho luthola esinye engingafuni ukusishicilela. Lesi sithombe sokugcina sichazwa ochungechungeni njengesifiphele. Bengifuna ukwazi ukuthi yiziphi izithombe ezibangela ukuphela kochungechunge nokuthi kungani. Ezimweni eziningi, ngokumangala kwami, kwakuphela ngenxa yokukhathazeka ngobumfihlo babanye abantu. Kwezinye izimo, kwakuphela ngenxa yezithombe zemibhalo yomsebenzi, amakhaya abanye abantu kanye nekhaya lami, njalonjalo.
 
Ngalolu hlelo, ngithole ukuthi imingcele yobumfihlo bami ifiphele kangakanani, uma ikhona nhlobo. Kodwa ngesikhathi esithile, kwadingeka nqume ukuthi ngishicilele isithombe noma cha. Kodwa lokhu kukhetha kwakuvame ukuba nzima futhi kungacaci.

[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Imisebe yamagagasi
 
 uJanuwari 2024 
 
 
 
Lo msebenzi uwumboniso wokuthi ukusabalala nokuphazamiseka kwamagagasi nokusebenzisana kwawo nendawo ezikuyo kungalingiswa kanjani, kusetshenziswa amamodeli ezibalo nokulingisa ngekhompyutha. Ukusetshenziswa kwamanani angahleliwe endaweni yemithombo yamagagasi, imvamisa yawo kanye nobukhulu bawo, kanye nokuqaliswa kokuncipha kobukhulu ngokwanda kwebanga ukusuka emthonjeni kanye nokubuyela emuva kwamagagasi emaphethelweni esikwele kanye nokuphazamiseka kwawo, kudala amaphethini athakazelisayo ngokubukeka abonisa ubunkimbinkimbi bokusebenzisana kwamagagasi endaweni elinganiselwe. Imiphumela iboniswa kusetshenziswa isikali sombala omnyama nomhlophe, esimelela amandla amagagasi ezindaweni ezahlukene zesikhala sesikwele.

Ikhodi yomthombo
 Ikhodi yomthombo ye-Python yalo msebenzi itholakala lapha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Isakhiwo sika-Recamán
SLUG: vystavy-recamanova-struktura
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/vystavy-recamanova-struktura/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: vystavy
DATE: 2023-12-14
SOURCE: src/content/posts/vystavy-recamanova-struktura.mdx
BUILT_HTML: vystavy-recamanova-struktura/index.html
============================================================
Isakhiwo sika-Recamán
 
 14. 9. 2023 - 31. 12. 2023, Jindřichův Hradec 
 
 Imyuziyamu yezithombe kanye nemidiya yezithombe yesimanje: Izithombe ezingaphezu kwezinombolo

Umkhondo wesikhombisi
 
 u-Agasti 2023 
 
 
 
Kungase kubonakale sengathi ukusebenzisana kwansuku zonke negundane lekhompyutha kuwumsebenzi ovamile futhi ongeyona into ebalulekile. Kodwa kuthiwani uma kungenzeka ukuguqula lezi zinyakazo eziyisijwayelo zibe okuthile okwengeziwe, okuthile okudala ukumelwa okubonakalayo okuyingqayizivele komsebenzi wekhompyutha? Ngenxa yekhodi ethatha indawo yesikhombisi segundane nombala wephikseli engaphansi kwaso, lo mbono ungafezeka.
 
Isebenza kanjani?
 Le khodi ilandelela lapho isikhombisi segundane sikhona esikrinini nokuthi yimuphi umbala wephikseli engaphansi kwaso. Lolu lwazi bese ludluliselwa ngesikhathi sangempela kukhansela elibonakalayo elinokulungiswa okufanayo nesikrini. Noma nini lapho igundane lihanjiswa, ikhodi idweba iphikseli enombala obungaphansi kwesikhombisi endaweni lapho isikhombisi sikhona. Lokhu kusho ukuthi njengoba umsebenzi wenziwa esikrinini futhi kuhanjiswa, imikhondo enemibala yakhiwa kukhansela. Le mikhondo iyingqayizivele futhi imele umsebenzi nokusebenzisana komsebenzisi nekhompyutha.
 
Kwenzekani ngemva kwesikhathi eside?
 Uma le nqubo iqhubeka isikhathi eside ngokwanele, ikhansela liqala ukugcwala futhi imikhondo iqala ukungabonakali kahle. Esikhundleni sayo, iningi lekhansela liqala ukubukeka njengomsindo ongahleliwe. Nokho, kunezindawo ezithile zesikrini ezingashintshi kakhulu ngokuhamba kwesikhathi. Lokhu kungaba, isibonelo, ibha engezansi yohlelo lwe-Windows, lapho kutholakala khona izithonjana zesoftware. Lezi zindawo kukhansela zihlala zifana kakhulu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Isiphetho
 Le khodi imele ukuhlangana kobuchwepheshe nobuciko, okwenza kube nokwenzeka ukuguqula ukusebenzisana kwansuku zonke nekhompyutha kube okuthile okubukeka kukhanga. Nakuba ekuqaleni kungase kubonakale sengathi ukusebenza nekhompyutha kuyisijwayelo futhi kuyisidina, lo msebenzi ubonisa ukuthi ngisho nomsebenzi onjalo ungaba nobuhle bawo nesigqi sawo.
 
Ikhodi yomthombo
 Ikhodi yomthombo ye-Python yalo msebenzi itholakala lapha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Amarekhodi ezitshalo
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin

LANGUAGE: zu

DATE: 2023-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zaznamy-rostlin.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zaznamy-rostlin/index.html
============================================================
Amarekhodi ezitshalo
 
 Juni 2023 
 
 
 
Isingeniso
 Kulesi sihloko, sizobheka iphrojekthi ehlanganisa umhlaba wezitshalo, ezobuchwepheshe, ukuhlela, kanye nobuciko obubonakalayo. Le phrojekthi isebenzisa i-Arduino ne-Python ukudala ukubona ngeso lengqondo okuhlangenwe nakho okuvela empilweni yezitshalo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | idiyagramu yokuxhuma i-arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Isebenza kanjani?
 I-Arduino ifakwe izinzwa ezintathu - i-thermometer, i-photoresistor, kanye ne-hygrometer. Lezi zinzwa zibekwe ebhodweni elinezinhlobo ezahlukene zezitshalo (isibonelo, ama-pattypan squash, uphizi, imbali yelanga, ama-eggplant, i-cress, upelepele, njll.). Izinzwa ziqoqa idatha ngokuqhubekayo mayelana nezinga lokushisa, ukukhanya, kanye nomswakama wenhlabathi.
 Uhlelo bese ludala ikhanvasi engenalutho elinezilinganiso ezingama-100×100 wamaphikseli. Iphikseli ngayinye kule khanvasi ifakwa umbala ngokusekelwe kudatha etholwe ezinzwa. Inani le-RGB lombala wephikseli linqunywa ngokuthi inani le-R (obomvu) lihambisana nezinga lokushisa, i-G (eluhlaza) ekukhanyeni, kanye ne-B (eluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka) kumswakama.
 
Ukubona ngeso lengqondo kosuku olulodwa
 Okokuqala, sizobheka inguqulo elula yephrojekthi, lapho isithombe esisodwa simelela usuku olulodwa lokuphila kwezitshalo. Uhlelo luqala ngokufaka umbala kuphikseli yokuqala ekhoneni eliphezulu kwesobunxele futhi kancane kancane lufaka umbala kuphikseli ngayinye ngokwamanani amanje atholwe ezinzwa. Ngemva kokufaka umbala kuphikseli, uhlelo lulinda isikhashana ngaphambi kokuqhubeka kuphikseli elandelayo. Uma lufika ekupheleni komugqa, luthuthela ekuqaleni komugqa olandelayo luqhubeke nokufaka umbala. Le nqubo iyaqhubeka kuze kube yilapho wonke amaphikseli ekhanvasi efakwe imibala.

Ukuhlanganisa okukhiphayo
 Ngemva kokubona ngeso lengqondo kosuku olulodwa, singayandisa le nqubo futhi sidale uchungechunge lwezithombe ezimelela isikhathi eside.
 Okukhiphayo okuyisikwele ngakunye kungabekwaphansi komunye nomunye, okudala izithombe ezihlanganisiwe. Njengoba ukukhiqizwa kokukhiphayo okulandelayo kuqala ngokushesha ngemva kokuqedwa kokukodwa, akukho ukuphazamiseka ngokuhamba kwesikhathi. Ngale ndlela, singadala izithombe ezinokulungiswa okungama-100x500 wamaphikseli, isibonelo, uma zakhiwe ngokukhiphayo okuhlanu.

 Ukubambezeleka phakathi kokulinganisa amaphikseli ngamanye kungandiswa. Ngale ndlela, ifomethi eyisikwele eyodwa engama-100×100 wamaphikseli ingamelela hhayi usuku olulodwa kuphela, kodwa izinsuku eziningi. Lo mgomo usivumela ukuba silandele ukukhula kwesitshalo esikhathini eside esithombeni esisodwa.

Ukuhlanganiswa kwemigomo
 Ngokuhlanganisa yomibili le migomo, singadala uchungechunge lwezithombe lapho isithombe ngasinye simelela izinsuku ezimbalwa futhi lonke uchungechunge luhlanganisa izinsuku eziningi noma amasonto. Ngale ndlela, singakwazi ukulandelela nokubona ngeso lengqondo ukukhula kwesitshalo esikhathini eside.

Ukuhunyushwa kwemiphumela
 Izithombe eziwumphumela zisinika umbono oyingqayizivele wokuphila kwezitshalo. “Imigqa” eluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka esithombeni imelela ubusuku, ngoba bekumanzi kakhulu, kodwa kungashisi kakhulu futhi bekungekho ukukhanya. Ngakolunye uhlangothi, “imigqa” ephuzi ibangelwa ukuthi ekupheleni kosuku, ilanga eliqondile laqala ukukhanya esitshalweni, okwabangela ukwanda kwezinga lokushisa nokukhanya. Lokhu kwabonakala esithombeni njengokwanda kwenani lesiteshi esibomvu nesiluhlaza, okuthi uma kuhlanganisiwe kwakha umbala ophuzi. Kwesinye isikhathi, imigqa ebuthakathaka iyabonakala ekuqaleni kobusuku, njengoba ngivula isibani ekamelweni kusihlwa. Kokunye okukhiphayo, umuntu angabona ekwehleni kokukhanya ukuthi, isibonelo, bekuna.
 
Ikhodi yomthombo
 Ikhodi yomthombo ye-Python yale phrojekthi itholakala lapha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Isiphetho
 Le phrojekthi iyisibonelo esihle sokuthi singayisebenzisa kanjani ubuchwepheshe ukuze siqonde kangcono futhi sibone ngeso lengqondo izinqubo zemvelo. Izithombe eziwumphumela zisinika umbono wokuphila kwezitshalo kanye nobunzima bezinto ezizwayo.
 
Kubalulekile ukuqaphela ukuthi nakuba le phrojekthi inikeza umbono oyingqayizivele wokuhlangenwe nakho kwezitshalo, ayihlanganisi zonke izici zokubona kwazo. Izitshalo ziyakwazi ukubona izinhlobo eziningi zezimpawu ezivela endaweni ezikuyo, kuhlanganise nomswakama womoya, isiqondiso samandla adonsela phansi, ukwakheka kwamakhemikhali enhlabathi, inkambu ye-electromagnetic, kanye nokucindezeleka komshini. Le phrojekthi ayizami ukurekhoda okuhlangenwe nakho okuphelele kwesitshalo, kodwa kunalokho igxila ezintweni ezintathu ezibalulekile ezingakalwa kalula futhi zibonakale.
 
Asinayo imbono yokuthi izitshalo ziyihumusha kanjani le datha, kodwa siyazi ukuthi ziyasabela kuyo. Le phrojekthi umzamo wokuhumusha lokhu kuhlangenwe nakho kube yifomethi esingayiqonda kangcono futhi siyibone ngeso lengqondo.

============================================================

TITLE: Ukuphindaphinda kuhlelo lwe-MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Ukuphindaphinda kuhlelo lwe-MS Word
 
 uLwezi 2022 
 
 
 
Ngithole indlela lapho inqubo yokuphindaphinda ingaqaliswa khona kuhlelo lwe-Microsoft Word. Kudingeka izinto ezimbili – umsebenzi wokuxhumanisa kanye netafula. Ngomsebenzi wokuxhumanisa, okuqukethwe kwedokhumenti A kungafakwa kudokhumenti B ngendlela yokuthi uma okuqukethwe kushintshwa kudokhumenti A, lezo zinguquko zibonakale ngokuzenzakalelayo kudokhumenti B. Ngamanye amazwi – okuqukethwe kwedokhumenti B kubhekisela kokuqukethwe kwedokhumenti A. Ukuphindaphinda kungaqaliswa ngokudala okuqukethwe okuzibhekisela kukho uqobo. Lokhu kungafinyelelwa kanjani? Kudingeka nje ukudala idokhumenti A (masiyibize ngokuthi doc A.docx) kanye nedokhumenti B (doc B.docx), kudokhumenti A udale itafula elingenalutho, ulikopishe bese ulinamathisela ngokuxhumene kudokhumenti B. Leli tafula elisanda kufakwa kufanele likopishwe kudokhumenti B bese linamathiselwa ngokuxhumene kokuqukethwe kwetafula kudokhumenti A. Lokhu kudala itafula eliqala ukuzifaka kulo uqobo kancane kancane. Ngokuvamile, i-MS Word iqala ukubambeka ize iphahlazeke ngokuphelele ekugcineni. Yonke le nqubo ingabonakala kuvidiyo engezansi.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Inkondlo-mbumbulu
SLUG: pseudobasen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pseudobasen/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Inkondlo-mbumbulu
 
 uMeyi 2022 
 
 
 
Wonke amagama akule nkondlo elandelayo (okungenani ngokwazi kwethu nangaphandle kwamagama asebenzayo) angamagama mbumbulu; ngakho-ke, lawa ngamagama okungenzeka ukuthi akhona ngokuhambisana nemithetho yemisindo yolimi lwesiCzech – lezi zinhlanganisela zemisindo kungenzeka kuleyo ndimi – kodwa awanikezwanga incazelo futhi awasetshenziswa. Ngakho-ke, inkondlo ayinangqondo ngokuyisisekelo, futhi noma ikuphi ukuhunyushwa kuncike ngokuphelele emcabangweni womfundi, nakuba imvelo yemisindo ethile yamagama mbumbulu ivusa lokho kucabanga kakhulu.
 
Lo mzamo awuzange uhlose ukudala umbhalo wezinkondlo onenani eliphezulu lobuciko, kodwa ukudlala nje ngamandla okuhlanganisa olimi lwendabuko. Kuyaphawuleka ukuthi kwigama ngalinye mbumbulu, umuntu angakwazi ukuhlonza ngokuthembekile isigaba samagama kanye nezigaba eziningi zohlelo lolimi.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Nakuba inkondlo ingeyona eyangempela ngokomgomo, lapho ihunyushwa, kungase kuvele umbono othile engqondweni yomfundi. Besifuna ukwazi ukuthi ubuhlakani bokwenziwa (AI) bungaba nawuphi umbono ngayo. Ngakho-ke, sisebenzise izinhlobo ezimbalwa zobuhlakani bokwenziwa ezitholakalayo (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion kanye ne-Night Café), ezikwazi ukudala okukhiphayo okuyisithombe kusetshenziswa okokufaka kombhalo. Uhlobo ngalunye luhlukile kancane, ezinye zikhiqiza okukhiphayo okufana nempilo, ezinye zicashile kakhulu, kanti ezinye zinesitayela sobuciko. Ziphinde zihluke ngokuthi zisiqonda kangakanani isiCzech (isibonelo, imodeli ye-ru-DALL-E isiqonda kahle kakhulu isiCzech).
 
Izingxenye ezihlukene zale nkondlo zanikezwa izinhlobo ezahlukene zobuhlakani bokwenziwa, futhi ngayinye yazo yakha uchungechunge lwayo oluyingqayizivele lwemibono esekelwe ekuhunyushweni kwezingxenye ezithile. Kulolu chungechunge ngalunye, sakha ividiyo lapho imibono ngayinye iboniswa khona, kuyilapho ingxenye efanele yenkondlo ifundwa. Imodeli ye-Deep Daze ikwazi ngisho nokurekhoda inqubo yokudala imibono, ngakho-ke kuvidiyo umuntu angabona ukuthi umbono wayo wakheka kanjani futhi wacaca kanjani kancane kancane.
 
ruDALL-E
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/CniEMECxcgw]
 
 
Dream
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MIS05zpqcWA]
 
 
starryai (Altair)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/qsmicN46yBk]
 
 
starryai (Orion)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/JDU4Zi6D9rE]
 
 
Night Café
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/txD8apt8CJo]
 
 
Deep Daze
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/m06W3aQXKUs]
 
 
Yini inkondlo evusa kuwe? Ingabe umbono wakho ngenkondlo uyahambisana nokuhunyushwa kobuhlakani bokwenziwa?
 
umbhali wenkondlo:
 Filip Drábek
 
umbhali wesithombe esihambisana nayo:
 Vojta Maur

TITLE: Umlando omfushane wokuphila eMhlabeni
SLUG: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi

SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

============================================================
Umlando omfushane wokuphila eMhlabeni
 
 Mashi 2021 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/1R8M4nRLPWQ]
 
 
Le vidiyo yenziwa njengomsebenzi wesikole eSikoleni Semfundo Ephakeme Yezohwebo, Ubuciko Obusetshenziswayo kanye Nokuklama e-Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Isithombe esisodwa kule vidiyo simelela 200 000 iminyaka yomlando woMhlaba. Lokhu kulinganiselwa ekubeni khona kohlobo lwe-Homo sapiens. Yingakho umuntu eboniswa kuphela esithombeni sokugcina kule vidiyo.
 
Zonke izinto ezibukwayo ezisetshenziswe kuvidiyo zathathwa ngumbhali.
