TITLE: Umbala, isimo kanye nethoni
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Umbala, isimo kanye nethoni
 
 obomvu 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | ithiyori yombala - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
Ngokomculi uWassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] kanye nethiyori yakhe yombala, kukhona ukuxhumana phakathi kombala nesimo. Kumodeli yombala i-RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] (obomvu, ophuzi, oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka) eyayisetshenziswa ngaleso sikhathi, wabelana ngezimo ezintathu 2D zejometri ngobudlelwano obulandelayo: unxantathu = ophuzi, isikwele = obomvu, umbuthano = oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka. Ngiqhutshwe umsebenzi wakhe, ngidale inguqulo yesimanje yale thiyori yombala. Esikhundleni semodeli yombala i-RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] okungenzeka ukuthi isiphelelwe yisikhathi namuhla, ngisebenzise imodeli yombala i-RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (obomvu, oluhlaza okotshani, oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka) enembile ngokomzimba futhi esetshenziswa kakhulu namuhla. Isikwele esasetshenziswa nguKandinsky ngisishintshe ngesimo esinamacala amahlanu (pentagon), ngoba isikwele sifana kakhulu nonxantathu kunombuthano, kanti isimo esinamacala amahlanu sibonakala njengoshintsho olufanele phakathi konxantathu nombuthano.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | ithiyori yombala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Ukuze ngithole isimo esifanele (isimo esinamacala amahlanu) phakathi konxantathu nombuthano, ngisebenzise ama-engeli angaphakathi ezimo. I-engeli yangaphakathi yonxantathu olinganayo ingu-60° (noma 60^1). I-engeli yangaphakathi yombuthano kufanele ibe ngu-180° (noma 60^1.268324336648371). Yebo, umbuthano awunazo izinhlangothi, ngakho-ke awukwazi ukuba nama-engeli angaphakathi, kodwa isibonelo, i-engeli yangaphakathi yesimo esinamacala ayinkulungwane ingu-179,64° futhi lesi simo sibukeka sifana kakhulu nombuthano. Uma ngithatha isilinganiso salezi zinombolo ezimbili 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] ngithola umphumela ongu-103.923°. Le-engeli yangaphakathi ifana kakhulu nesimo esinamacala amahlanu, esine-engeli yangaphakathi engu-108°. Ukuze nginwebe le thiyori yombala, ngisebenzise inqubo efanayo ukwengeza izinguquko phakathi kwezimo eziyisisekelo zombala ngobudlelwano: isikwele = ophuzi, isimo esinamacala ayisishiyagalombili = oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka (cyan). Ngizamile ukugcwalisa le thiyori ngokwabela izimo ezinemibala ekuphakameni kwethoni ngokobudlelwano: i-engeli ebukhali + umbala ofudumele = ithoni ephezulu, i-engeli eyisicaba + umbala obandayo = ithoni ejulile. Lo mqondo ngiwurekhode emugqeni womculo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | ithiyori yombala enwetshiwe - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Ngizamile ukugcwalisa le thiyori ngokwabela izimo ezinemibala ekuphakameni kwethoni ngokobudlelwano: i-engeli ebukhali + umbala ofudumele = ithoni ephezulu, i-engeli eyisicaba + umbala obandayo = ithoni ejulile. Lo mqondo ngiwurekhode emugqeni womculo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | ithiyori yomculo wombala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Umbala owodwa engingakwazanga ukuwabela noma yisiphi isimo noma ithoni umbala onsomi (magenta). Kufanele ufane noluhlaza okotshani (nawo ungaphakathi phakathi koluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka nobomvu futhi ubuye ube obandayo nofudumele ngesikhathi esisodwa). Kufanele ubukeke njengombuthano oyingxenye? Noma njengonxantathu oyindilinga? Noma kufanele ufane noluhlaza okotshani - ngakho-ke ube yisimo esinamacala amahlanu? Kuze kube manje angikayitholi impendulo yalo mbuzo.

TITLE: Ukubona komuntu
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Ukubona komuntu
 
 okubomvu 2019 
 
 
 
Ukubona komuntu empeleni kwehlukile kulokho abantu abaningi abakucabangayo. Idatha yasekuqaleni ekhiqizwa amehlo ethu ihluke kakhulu kulokho ubuchopho bethu obusihumusha ngakho kamuva, ngakho-ke indlela esicabanga ukuthi siyabona ngayo. Isithombe esingezansi sibonisa "idatha ehlanzekile" ekhululiwe ekulungisweni "kwangemva kokukhiqiza" ngobuchopho bethu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | ukubona komuntu okubonakalayo]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
Esithombeni, umuntu angaqaphela ukungapheleli okumbalwa okugqamile:
 
 
- Isithombe siphendulwe ngokuzungeza ngokuvundlile nangokuqondile. Ingane ngayinye ngemva kokuzalwa ibona ngale ndlela, kodwa ngokuhamba kwesikhathi ubuchopho bufunda ukuguqula isithombe. Lo mphumela ungase futhi wenzeke kancane kakhulu ngemva kokudla izidakamizwa ezibangela ukudideka kwengqondo.
 
- Isithombe siboniswa iso ngalinye ngokwehlukana - ubuchopho kuphela obusihlanganisela esithombeni esisodwa.
 
- Isithombe sibukhali kuphela engxenyeni encane ephakathi nendawo futhi siba nokufiphala uma sibheka ezinhlangothini.
 
- Isithombe sinemibala kakhulu engxenyeni encane ephakathi nendawo futhi silahlekelwa ukugcwala kwemibala uma sibheka ezinhlangothini. Lokhu kubangelwa inani elikhulu lezinhlayiya ezibona imibala (cones) phakathi neso, elincipha ngokushesha uma lisuka phakathi nendawo. Esonke iso kunechashazi elimnyama elilodwa - okuthiwa yindawo eyimpumputhe (ngokobuchwepheshe i-scotoma). Kule ngxenye yeso azikho izinhlayiya ezibona imibala noma izinhlayiya ezibona ukukhanya (rods), ngoba lapho kutholakala khona umzwa wamehlo. Ubuchopho buvame ukugcwalisa lezi zindawo eziyimpumputhe ngolwazi oluvela kwelinye iso.
 
- Imigqa eqondile igobile kancane ibe yimigqa eyindilinga njengokungathi kukhona umphumela welensi yezinhlanzi (fisheye) noma ilensi yekhamera ebanzi. Ubuchopho buzama ukuqondisa le migqa, kodwa lokhu kuyinkohliso nje.
 
- Ekuboneni kwethu, nempumulo iyabonakala, leyo ubuchopho obuvame ukuyihlunga.
 
- Isithombe asihlanzekile ngokuphelele, kodwa siqukethe umsindo obonakalayo obuthakathaka. Lokhu kwakhiwa inhlanganisela yomsindo womzimba wokukhanya, umsindo wama-photoreceptor uqobo, kanye neminye imisebenzi yezinzwa yohlelo lokubona. Ngaphansi kwezimo ezijwayelekile, ubuchopho buvame ukukucindezela, kodwa kungase kubonakale ikakhulukazi ebumnyameni, lapho amehlo evaliwe, noma ezindaweni eziyisicaba.
 
 
Emsebenzini wami wokugcina wonyaka wesibili womkhakha wokudala imidiya eminingi esifundweni sokugqwayiza (animation) eSikoleni Sobuchwepheshe Sezohwebo, Ubuciko Obusetshenziswayo kanye Nokuklama, ngenze ividiyo elingisa izinkinga ezahlukahlukene zamehlo ezithakazelisayo:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

ITanzania 2019
 
Izithombe, imibhalo
 
 
 
Le nethulo isifinyezo semibono ehlukahlukene kanye nokuqonda engikutholile ngesikhathi ngihlala isikhashana eTanzania. Le mibono nemizwa ngiyiveza ngezithombe nangombhalo.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Ngizama ukusiza abantu bendawo ngomsebenzi wami, ngandise ukuqwashisa ngezinkinga zabo ukuze kuqedwe imibono ethile yobuhlanga. Futhi ngikholelwa ukuthi iYurophu kufanele ithathe isibonelo kuleli zwe ezintweni eziningi.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
ETanzania kukhona amaqembu amabili amakhulu enkolo - ubuSulumane nobuKristu. Nakuba ukuhlalisana kwala maqembu amabili kungase kube nzima ukukucabanga kumuntu waseYurophu omaphakathi wekhulu le- 21, amaSulumane namaKristu endawo lapha ahlala ngokuthula futhi ayahloniphana.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Mina ngokwami angibathandi abantu abagqoke imifaniswano (amaphoyisa, amasosha, onogada...). Okungenani hhayi eCzech Republic. Ngikholelwa ukuthi ngamandla abanikezwe wona, ukuzikhukhumeza kwabo kuyakhula. Nokho, lokhu kungathandi kwami imifaniswano bekungewona opodstatněný.Lid eTanzania. Labo abebegqoke imifaniswano lapho bangiphathe kahle futhi bahlale bengisiza.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
Abantu bendawo bangiphathe kahle kakhulu futhi banobungane, ngisho nangaphandle kwezindawo zezivakashi. Uma ngivakashela edolobhaneni lendawo, abantu bangibingelela ngokuthi "Jambo!" (Okusho ukuthi "Sawubona!"). Abaningi babo baze bakhuluma nami.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
"Laba bantu abavela ezindaweni ezishisayo bangibonakala bejabule kakhulu kunathi" (ingxenye yengxoxo phakathi kwabagibeli abambalwa engiyizwile endizeni ngesikhathi ngibuyela e-CR)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Ngenxa yokwanda komhlaba wonke, ezokuvakasha nazo ziyavela eZanzibar, eziletha izinzuzo eziningi, kodwa nezinkinga. Izinzuzo eziyinhloko ukukhula komnotho kanye nokukhuphuka kwezinga lemfundo. Enye inzuzo ukwanda kokuqwashisa ngaleli zwe emhlabeni, kodwa ngeshwa le nzuza ivame ukuphikwa izindonga ezinkulu zezindawo zamahhotela, izivakashi eziningi ezingalokothi zizidlule. Ngibona ukubhubha kwamasiko endawo njengento engalungile. Isibonelo sale nkinga ukufuduka kwamaMasai amaningi esuka ezweni laseTanzania (ikakhulukazi endaweni ezungeze iKilimanjaro) eya ezindaweni ezinabavakashi abaningi (emabhishi aseZanzibar). Lawa maMasai athengisa izinto ezincane lapha futhi ayisikhangiso sezivakashi nje. AmaMasai akholelwa ukuthi uma othile ewathatha isithombe, uyabe eba umphefumulo wawo. Ezindaweni zezivakashi, nokho, bazivumela ukuthathwa izithombe ngamasenti ambalwa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

Isithombe somuntu
 
 Disemba 2018 
 
 
 
Lo msebenzi wadalwa njengomsebenzi wesikole wesifundo sezithombe eSikoleni esiphakeme sobuchwepheshe sezohwebo, ubuciko obusetshenziswayo kanye nokuklama e-Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Inhloso kwakuwukuhlanganisa ngokulinga izithombe zabantu, izinhlobo zobuntu bengqondo, ukuqagela kwesithombe, ukugqwayiza kanye nomsindo. Ukubukeka kokugcina kwezithombe namavidiyo kwakusekelwe ekuhlolweni kobuntu buka-Eysenck kanye nokuhlukaniswa kwemizwa ngokwe-quadrant ka-Galen.

TITLE: Igridi yebhodi lesheke
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Igridi yebhodi lesheke
 
 Disemba 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Esithombeni esiyisibonelo, ngisebenzise igridi yebhodi lesheke, lapho isikwele esisodwa sebhodi lesheke silingana ne-pixel eyodwa yesithombe. Izindawo ezimhlophe zesikwele sebhodi lesheke ngemuva kokusebenzisa le gridi yiwona ma-pixel okuqala esithombe, kanti izindawo ezimnyama zihlukile ngombala nangokukhanya. Isithombe esigcwele sibukeka njengendawo empunga enombala owodwa, kodwa kuphela ngemva kokusondeza kwedijithali lapho isithombe sokuqala sibonakala khona. Ukuze ubonise kahle kusiqaphi se-Full HD, isithombe kufanele sibe cishe 2000 px ukuphakama. Ukuboniswa okungalungile kungenzeka uma uvula isithombe kusiphequluli esenza kabusha igridi ye-pixel ngendlela ye-bicubic. Isiphequluli esiqinisekisiwe, ngokwesibonelo, i-Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Njengesinye isibonelo sokusetshenziswa kwalo mgomo, ngidale isithombe esiqukethe umehluko wombala kanye nencazelo.

Ukuhlanekezelwa kolwazi
 
 u-Agasti 2018 
 
 
 
Mangaki amanxusa adlula kuwo umbhalo esiwubona ku-inthanethi ngaphambi kokuba ufike kithi? Ingabe iqiniso lawo lithembekile? Umhlaba esiwubona ngezibuko zezingcingo zethu, amakhompyutha kanye nomabonakude uhluke kangakanani?
 
Kubalulekile ukuqinisekisa lonke ulwazi esilubonayo emithonjeni eminingi futhi singathembeli eqinisweni lalo.
 
Lo msebenzi wenziwa ngokuthatha isithombe esisodwa bese uphinda uthatha izithombe zaleso sithombe esifanayo esikrinini sekhompyutha.

EMexico 2018

TITLE: Imigqi emibili
SLUG: dva-rytmy
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dva-rytmy/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dva-rytmy.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
Imigqi emibili
 
 u-Ephreli 2018 
 
 
 
Ividiyo yokuqala iqukethe imigqi emibili: ukubaneka okubonakalayo nokuchofoza okuzwakalayo. Le migqi emibili inobude besikhathi obuhluke kancane. Ngalokhu kuchezuka okuncane, ividiyo ifinyelela ezingeni eliphezulu lokungakhululeki engxenyeni ethile. Kuvidiyo yesibili, ngingezelele izinto ezibukwayo ezigxile endikimbeni elwa nempi kulo mqondo oyisisekelo.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | umqondo oyisisekelo]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
Imigqi emibili 1 - mnyama x mhlophe, umqondo oyisisekelo
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | ukusetshenziswa komqondo oyisisekelo]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg] 
Imigqi emibili 2 - ukusetshenziswa komqondo oyisisekelo ezintweni ezibukwayo

TITLE: Izinombolo ezingahleliwe ezikhethwe ngumuntu
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Izinombolo ezingahleliwe ezikhethwe ngumuntu
 
 u-Ephreli 2018 
 
 
 
Kulo msebenzi, kwahanjiswa uhlu lwemibuzo kubantu abambalwa. Kulolu hlu lwemibuzo, abantu abahloliwe babhala izinombolo eziyikhulu ezingahleliwe ebangeni 1-100, ezabafika engqondweni. Ngemuva kwalokho, ngadlulisa le datha kukhompyutha futhi ngadala igrafu eyodwa kusuka kuhlu lwemibuzo ngalunye. I-eksisi evundlile x inquma ukulandelana kwezinombolo ngazinye kanti i-eksisi emile y inquma inani lezinombolo ezikhethiwe. Igrafu yombala oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka idalwe yikhompyutha, okungukuthi, umkhiqizi wezinombolo ezingahleliwe. Ekuqhathanisweni okuphelele kwayo yonke imigrafu, umuntu angaqaphela ukuthi umuntu ngamunye ohloliwe unegrafu eyingqayizivele nendlela yakhe yokufaka izinombolo. Kungenzeka futhi ukuqaphela ukuthi emigrafini eminingi, imijikelezo ethile ecishe ifane iyaphindaphindeka (ikakhulukazi ngendlela yokukhuphuka nokwehla kwezinombolo kancane kancane).
 
IZINOMBOLO EZIKHETHWE NGUMKHIQIZI WEZINOMBOLO EZINGAHLELIWE

============================================================
Ukuphefumula okungajwayelekile
 
 Mashi 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]

SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: zu

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Ngokombono wegobolondo
 
 Mashi 2018 
 
 
 
Lawa mavidiyo agxile ekuboneni kwegobolondo ukuthi lizizwa kanjani futhi lidlula kuphi? Umbono walo ngomhlaba awubalulekile ngaphansi kowomuntu! Kuvidiyo yokuqala ungabona ukuqoshwa kwasekuqaleni kolwandle. Kuvidiyo yesibili, ohlangothini lwesobunxele lwesikrini, umbala omaphakathi wozime ngamunye wokuqoshwa kwasekuqaleni uyavezwa. Engxenyeni yesokudla yesikrini kukhona igrafu ye-RGB echaza lo mbala.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

Isikhunta
 
 uLwezi 2017 
 
 
 
Lo mjikelezo wezithombe ubhala phansi isikhunta esencwadini yami yokufunda ulimi lwesiJalimane. Izithombe zihlelwe ngokulandelana kwazo njengoba amakhasi encwadi elandelana. Ngaleyo ndlela, ukwakheka okungahleliwe, okuthakazelisayo nokuyizilingo kungena esithombeni ngasinye.

SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Ukuphazamiseka kwegridi evamile
 
 Okthoba 2017 
 
 
 
Kulo msebenzi, bengizama ukudala uchungechunge lwemisebenzi yobuciko engabonakali. Ngihlukanise idokhumenti izingxenye ezine. Engxenyeni yokuqala, ngidale igridi eyisisekelo evamile, engxenyeni yesibili ngiqhubeke ngale gridi futhi ngaqala ukuyiphazamisa kancane, okubangele ukungazethembi. Engxenyeni yesithathu, ngiphazamise le gridi yejometri evamile ngezimo ezingajwayelekile eziphikisanayo. Futhi engxenyeni yesine yokugcina, ngigqwayize lezi zimo eziphilayo.
 
Ingxenye yokuqala:
 IGIDI EYISISEKELO
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Ukungabonakali 1.0 - igridi evamile]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg]

Isilinganiso sombala
 
 u-Ephreli 2017 
 
 
 
Engxenyeni engakwesobunxele yesithombe kukhona 1156 izithombe ezikugridi engu-34x34 px. Iphikseli ngayinye enemibala imele umbala omaphakathi wesithombe ngasinye esivela kuleso sifunda. Engxenyeni engakwesokudla yesithombe kukhona umbala omaphakathi wawo wonke amaphikseli avela engxenyeni engakwesobunxele, okungukuthi, umbala omaphakathi wazo zonke izithombe ezivela kuleso sifunda.

TITLE: Izicucu zoshukela
SLUG: kostky-cukru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/kostky-cukru/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/kostky-cukru.mdx

============================================================
Izicucu zoshukela
 
 uNdasa 2017 
 
 
 
Lolu chungechunge lwezithombe luthathwe ngesikhathi sokuhlolwa kwethalente eSikoleni Semfundo Ephakeme Yezohwebo, Ubuciko Obusetshenziswayo kanye Nokuklama e-Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Engxenyeni yezithombe zokuhlolwa, besivumelekile ukuletha izinto zethu, kodwa inani nosayizi wazo kwakulinganiselwe. Ngaletha ibhokisi lezicucu zoshukela, engazihlela epuletini ngendlela ehlelekile bese ngizithela ngamanzi. Izicucu zancibilika kancane kancane, zawa, futhi zawela ngokungahleliwe.

TITLE: Umtapo wezobuciko basemgwaqweni
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Umtapo wezobuciko basemgwaqweni
 
 Mashi 2017 
 
 
 
Kuyo yonke indawo esizungezile kukhona imisebenzi eminingi ethokozisayo yobuciko basemgwaqweni kusukela ezitika ezincane, izindonga ezidwetshiwe kuya ezinhlotsheni ezahlukene zezinto 3D. Ngenxa yokuthi abadali bale misebenzi kumele bahlale bengaziwa ngenxa yokungqubuzana nomthetho, lo mhlaba wobuciko basemgwaqweni awunabugovu ngokuphelele futhi uhlukene nomhlaba wezentengiso. Ubuciko basemgwaqweni esingabubona cishe yonke indawo edolobheni buvame ukuhlala busezingeni eliphezulu. Ubuciko obusha basemgwaqweni buvame ukumboza obudala, upende uyaxebuka kanye nodonga, izinto 3D ziba yizisulu zokucekelwa phansi kanti izitika ziyaxebuka noma ziphele amandla ngenxa yezimo zezulu ngokuhamba kwesikhathi. Kuyinqubo yemvelo, isici sesikhathi, nokho ngikholelwa ukuthi obunye ubuciko basemgwaqweni bufanelwe ukunwetshwa impilo yabo.
 
Yingakho-ke nginqume ukubhala imisebenzi ethokozisayo. Ngenxa yale njongo, ngasungula ikhasi le-Facebook elibizwa ngokuthi i-Street art library ngonyaka ka 2017, lapho ngisafaka khona okuthunyelwe kuze kube manje. Ukuze ngifinyelele kubantu abaningi, ngasungula ikhasi elinegama elifanayo ku-Instagram.
 
Kula makhasi, ngizama ukwengeza ikakhulukazi imisebenzi ephethe umqondo othile, kanti ingxenye yobuhle bomsebenzi ngiyibona njengento yesibili.
 
Umtapo wezobuciko basemgwaqweni (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Umtapo wezobuciko basemgwaqweni (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Isithombe sesikrini sekhasi le-Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Isithombe sesikrini sekhasi le-Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Uhlelo lokusebenza lwe-Google
 
 uLwezi 2016 
 
 
 
Le vidiyo emfushane iyinkomba yezimvume ezingenangqondo zohlelo lokusebenza lweselula lwe-Google (lolu hlelo lokusebenza alufakwe ngaphambilini kumafoni akwa-Samsung kuphela, futhi alukwazi nokukhipheka). Uhlolojikelele lwezimvume lungatholakala ocingweni olusebenzisa isistimu yokusebenza ye-Android ku-Nastavení/Správce aplikací/STAŽENÉ/Aplikace Google.

SLUG: vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2

LANGUAGE: zu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: vystavy
DATE: 2016-08-14
SOURCE: src/content/posts/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2.mdx
BUILT_HTML: vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2/index.html
============================================================
Akunandaba ukuthi uvela kusiphi isifunda...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, i-Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): I-Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. Disemba 2014 e-Mombasa ngathatha isithombe sodonga oludala olubhalwe phansi. Ngemuva kwalokho ngazama ukuhumusha umbhalo okulo. Ingxenye eyodwa efundekayo ngiyifake enjinini yokusesha ye-inthanethi ngase ngithola isithombe esilandelayo.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Umbiko ovela ku-Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

============================================================
Ubuso bamanzi
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, e-Plzeň 
 
 Igalari yedolobha lase-Plzeň: NGAPHAMBI, MANJE, NGEMVA 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Impilo yethu iku-inthanethi neyempilo yangempela yehluke kangakanani? Kungani uphumela ngaphandle uma singathola yonke into ku-Google? Ulwazi oluvela ku-inthanethi luhlanekezelwe futhi aluhlangene kangako neqiniso.
 Ziyisibuko salokho esifuna ukukubona.
 Ilanga libonakala ebusweni bamanzi, liphrojethwe odongeni futhi libonakale esibukweni... Ingabe lifike kithi lingashintshile ngempela?
 
Ukuqoqwa kombukiso:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmsbQt0g9nc
 
Okuningi ngombukiso kumasayithi e-Galerie města Plzně:
 http://galerie-plzen.cz/vystavy/vystavy-2016/predtim-ted-potom

TITLE: Kusuka edolobheni kuya kwingozi

SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Kusuka edolobheni kuya kwingozi
 
 uJanuwari 2016 
 
 
 
Ngo-2016 ngathola umsebenzi wokwenza isitayela sedolobha njengengxenye yokulungiselela isikole sobuciko e-ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14. Bengifuna ukwazi ukuthi lesi sitayela singafika kuphi, ngakho-ke ngisebenze kulo msebenzi iminyaka elandelayo kuze kube manje (unyaka ka-2019). Kusukela esitayeleni sedolobha esilula, ngidlulele ekuphazamiseni amagridi avamile, okubangele ukungezwani okumangalisayo ngokombono wobuciko. Ngaphezu kwalokho, ngizamile ukusebenzisa ithuba kulo msebenzi, kodwa angikwazanga ukuthola lutho olunjengengozi yangempela ngaphandle kwesizathu. Ukuqina (determinism) bekuhlale kungivimba, ngakho-ke bengisebenzisa kuphela ithuba elingelona iqiniso (isizathu singanqunywa, kodwa umphumela kunzima ukuwubikezela ngakho-ke ubonakala ungozi). Ezinye izindlela zangiholela ekupheleni komgwaqo ngakho-ke kwadingeka ngifune omunye umbono nesinye isixazululo sezithombe, isib. ukuhlela okukhiphayo okubonakalayo, umphumela we-moiré, ukulandelana kukaFibonacci, njll. Uchungechunge lwezithombe ezingezansi luyisifinyezo sale misebenzi.

Ukhula
 
 Meyi 2008 
 
 
 
Umsebenzi wasenkulisa.
 
Umsebenzi wawukusika izimbali eziqhakazayo ephepheni elinemibala bese wenza isithombe ngazo.
 
Esikhundleni sezimbali ngenze plevel.
